I would eat that all up!","type":"example"},{"text":"Most other people would do it differently, helping anybody who was in trouble, whether they knew them or not.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A modal verb, the subjunctive of will; usually followed by a bare infinitive.","Without explicit condition, or with loose or vague implied condition, indicating a hypothetical or imagined state or action."],"tags":["auxiliary"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I would ask you all to sit down.","type":"example"},{"text":"I would imagine that they have already left.","type":"example"},{"text":"I would say/think we would/might do better to catch the earlier flight.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A modal verb, the subjunctive of will; usually followed by a bare infinitive.","Suggesting conditionality or potentiality in order to express a sense of politeness, tentativeness, indirectness, hesitancy, uncertainty, etc."],"tags":["auxiliary"]},{"glosses":["A modal verb, the subjunctive of will; usually followed by a bare infinitive.","Used to express what the speaker would do in another person's situation, as a means of giving a suggestion or recommendation."],"tags":["auxiliary"]},{"examples":[{"text":"She looked as if she would be sick.","type":"example"},{"text":"He's very security-conscious, so he would have remembered to lock the door.","type":"example"},{"text":"They would be arriving in London round about now.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A modal verb, the subjunctive of will; usually followed by a bare infinitive.","Used to express the speaker's belief or assumption."],"tags":["auxiliary"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Sorry, officer, I wouldn't know anything about the crime, since I was nowhere near the scene.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A modal verb, the subjunctive of will; usually followed by a bare infinitive.","Could naturally be expected to (given the situation, the tendencies of someone's character etc.)."],"tags":["auxiliary"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Would you pass the salt, please?","type":"example"},{"text":"Just reach me down that file, would you.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A modal verb, the subjunctive of will;
